We investigate the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW) and compare solutions obtaining by exact solver SCIP with heuristic algorithms such as LKH, 2-OPT, 3-OPT, the ORTools framework, and the deep learning model JAMPR. We demonstrate that for problem of size 50 deep learning and classical heuristic solutions became close to SCIP exact solution but requires less time. Additionally for problems with size 100, SCIP exact methods around 13 times slower that neural and classical heuristics with the same route cost and on around 50% worse for the first feasible solution on the same time. To conduct experiments, we developed the Smart Routes platform for solving route optimization problems, which includes exact, heuristic, and deep learning models, and facilitates convenient integration of custom algorithms and datasets.

</details>


### [115] [Deep Reinforcement Learning solution for pickup and delivery routing problems with time window and capacity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14156)
*Andrew Soroka,Alex Meshcheryakov,Sergey Gerasimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将改进的JAMPR深度强化学习模型应用于带容量和时间窗约束的取送货问题（CPDPTW），在中小规模问题上能快速给出最优解，在更大规模（>200）问题上能快速给出次优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着全球城市人口增长，车辆取送货路线优化问题变得日益重要。虽然小规模问题可通过经典方法求解，但在现实世界约束（容量和时间窗）下，中大规模问题的快速（实时）路线优化仍然极具挑战性。

Method: 采用改进的JAMPR模型，一种深度强化学习方法，将其适配应用于带容量和时间窗约束的取送货问题（CPDPTW）。

Result: 获得了鲁棒的模型：对中小规模问题可给出快速最优解，对更大规模（>200）问题可给出快速次优解。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习方法（改进的JAMPR模型）首次成功应用于CPDPTW问题，在保证求解速度的同时兼顾了解的质量，为大规模实际场景下的实时路线优化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The task of constructing vehicles optimal routes for pickup and delivery of goods is one of most promising tasks in the context of global urban population growth. Although this kind of problems with small size can be solved by various classical approaches, a fast (or realtime) route optimizer under the constraints of the real world (such as capacity and time windows constraints) for medium-large size problems still remains a highly challenging task. In this work we, for the first time, successfully applied a deep Reinforcing Learning approach (modified JAMPR model) to solve Pickup and Delivery problem with Capacity and Time Window constraints (CPDPTW). We obtained a robust model that gives a fast optimal solution for problems of small and medium size, and gives fast suboptimal solution for problems of larger (> 200) size.

</details>


### [116] [Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14177)
*Xuanmian He,Can Li,Wanjing Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构引导的时空注意力图神经网络（SGSAN），通过显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG）来识别交通状态的不变宏观传播路径，并引入基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制将动态时空注意力锚定到结构先验上，同时设计解耦的两阶段优化框架解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的冲突，在多个真实数据集上实现最先进的预测精度并具备内建可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度时空模型在交通流预测中表现出色，但由于其固有的不透明性，在安全关键的城市系统部署中受到限制。现有事后诊断方法常受虚假相关性困扰，无法揭示模型内在的决策机制，导致可解释性欠佳和运营可信度受限。

Method: SGSAN包含三个核心组件：（1）显式学习静态有向依赖图（DDG），取代传统无约束自适应图，识别交通状态的不变宏观传播路径；（2）基于InfoNCE的软耦合机制，将模型的动态时空注意力锚定到结构先验上，提供决策机制的机理解释，同时防止模型过度依赖瞬态局部噪声；（3）解耦的两阶段优化框架，解决结构发现与预测误差最小化之间的根本冲突。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，SGSAN在预测精度上达到最先进水平，同时提供与交通网络物理逻辑有机对齐的内建可解释性。

Conclusion: SGSAN成功实现了高精度预测与内建可解释性的兼顾，其解释性与交通网络物理逻辑自然一致，为安全关键交通系统提供了更可信任的深度学习替代方案。

Abstract: Deep spatiotemporal models integrating graph convolutions and attention mechanisms have demonstrated excellent performance in network-level traffic flow prediction, owing to their exceptional ability to capture complex spatiotemporal dependencies. Despite their predictive success, deployment of such models in safety-critical urban systems remains constrained by their inherent lack of transparency. Existing post-hoc diagnostic methods often struggle with spurious correlations and fail to unveil the intrinsic decision-making mechanisms governing traffic dynamics, resulting in suboptimal interpretability and limited operational trustworthiness. To address these challenges, this paper proposes the Structure-Guided Spatiotemporal Attention Graph Neural Network (SGSAN). Departing from traditional architectures that rely on unconstrained adaptive graphs, SGSAN explicitly learns a static Directed Dependency Graph (DDG) to identify the invariant macroscopic propagation paths of traffic states. We further introduce an InfoNCE-based soft-coupling mechanism that anchors the model's dynamic spatiotemporal attention to this structural prior, offering a mechanistic account of the model's decision-making process while ensuring robust forecasting by aligning attention-based reasoning with identified macroscopic dependencies and preventing over-reliance on ephemeral local noise. Furthermore, a decoupled two-stage optimization framework is developed to resolve the fundamental conflict between structural discovery and predictive error minimization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that SGSAN achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while providing built-in interpretability that organically aligns with the physical logic of traffic networks.

</details>


### [117] [KV Cache Compression Through the Lens of Transform Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14191)
*Hannah Laus,Claudio Mayrink Verdun,Hao Wang,Flavio du Pin Calmon,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC)，一种针对KV缓存的新型量化方法。该方法从注意力感知的角度出发，证明在白噪声量化模型下，期望的注意力感知失真可分解为可跨token和通道分解的加性键值贡献，并基于变换编码和反向注水原理分配比特，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct及Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上实现约5.8倍压缩率下接近无损的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存存储历史token信息，是长上下文推理中的主要内存瓶颈。现有量化方法统一以低精度数据类型表示KV缓存，仅最小化缓存本身的重构误差，未考虑误差如何通过注意力机制传播。这种忽视注意力机制的量化策略在长上下文场景下可能造成显著的精度损失。

Method: 作者首先从理论层面证明：在白噪声量化模型下，期望的注意力感知失真可分解为键和值两个加性贡献，且可跨token与通道分解。在此基础上，借鉴信号处理与率失真理论中的变换编码（transform coding）和反向注水（reverse water-filling）经典工具，提出AATC方法，通过在标定集上分配比特来最小化注意力感知失真。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct上，跨越LongBench、RULER、GSM8K、MMLU-Pro和MATH-500多项基准评估，AATC在约5.8倍压缩率下实现接近无损的准确率，而各基线方法在至少部分设定下均出现精度退化。

Conclusion: 通过将率失真理论中的经典工具与注意力机制的结构特性相结合，AATC证明了注意力感知的量化策略能够显著优于统一低精度量化方法，在不影响精度的前提下实现高效KV缓存压缩。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache stores information from past tokens and is a major memory bottleneck in long-context inference. Existing quantization methods address this bottleneck by representing the KV cache uniformly with lower-precision data types and designing quantization schemes to minimize reconstruction error in the cache itself, without accounting for how that error propagates through attention mechanisms. We prove that, under a white-noise quantization model, the expected attention-aware distortion decomposes into additive key and value contributions that factor across tokens and channels. Building on transform coding and reverse water-filling, which are classical tools from signal processing and rate-distortion theory, we introduce Attention-Aware Transform Coding (AATC), which allocates bits over a calibration set to minimize attention-aware distortion. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen-2.5-7B-Instruct, evaluated across LongBench, RULER, GSM8K, MMLU-Pro, and MATH-500, our method achieves near-lossless accuracy at approximately $5.8\times$ compression, whereas each baseline degrades in at least some settings.

</details>


### [118] [MINT: A Universal Zero-Shot Predictor for Transaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14198)
*Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Viktor Drobnyi,Maeve Madigan,Julia Rozanova,David Sutton,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MINT（Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions），一种将预训练交易序列编码器与仅解码器LLM通过轻量级嵌入注入、交易-语言对齐和指令调优连接起来的框架，在分布内和分布外预测问答任务上达到最先进性能，同时大幅减少输入token、延迟和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行使用交易序列数据执行欺诈预防、信用风险评估等任务。现有支付基础模型虽能生成丰富的上下文嵌入，但缺乏跨新下游预测任务的灵活零样本推理能力；而基于LLM的现有方法未能充分挖掘交易数据的预测信号，且依赖昂贵的文本序列化或难以扩展的任务特定架构。

Method: MINT通过三个关键组件构建框架：1) 轻量级嵌入注入（embedding injection），将预训练交易序列编码器的紧凑交易嵌入输入到LLM；2) 交易-语言对齐（transaction-language alignment）；3) 指令调优（instruction tuning），使模型能够实现零样本预测问答。

Result: MINT在分布内和分布外问题场景下均达到最先进的预测问答性能，同时相对于文本序列化基线大幅减少了输入token、延迟和内存消耗。通过全面分析表示、对齐策略、训练数据和历史长度，证实紧凑交易嵌入比分文本序列化更优。

Conclusion: 紧凑交易嵌入是多模态推理和零样本预测任务中优于文本序列化的交易表示方案。MINT框架有效弥合了交易序列编码器与LLM之间的差距，实现了高效且可扩展的零样本交易预测。

Abstract: Banks analyse sequential financial transaction data to perform many tasks, including fraud prevention, credit risk assessment and offer personalization. To improve the predictive accuracy of these tasks, Payments Foundation Models encode transaction sequence data as rich contextual embeddings, which can then be provided to task-specific models as features. However, these Foundation Models are not designed for flexible zero-shot reasoning across novel downstream prediction tasks, limiting their adaptability and utility. Existing LLM-based approaches to zero-shot prediction often fail to fully exploit the predictive signal within transaction data, while relying on costly text serialization or task-specific architectures that scale poorly. To address these limitations, we present the Multimodal Instruction Network for Transactions (MINT), a framework that connects a pretrained transaction sequence encoder to a decoder-only LLM through lightweight embedding injection, transaction-language alignment, and instruction tuning. We find that MINT achieves state-of-the-art predictive question-answering performance in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution questions, while substantially reducing input tokens, latency, and memory consumption compared to text-serialization baselines. Through comprehensive analyses of representations, alignment strategies, training data, and history length, we establish that compact transaction embeddings are a superior approach to transaction representation than text serialization for multimodal reasoning and zero-shot prediction tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Adaptive Protection for Evolutionary Feature Construction in Symbolic Regression with Application to Credit Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14209)
*Hengzhe Zhang,Qi Chen,Bing Xue,Lean Yu,Wolfgang Banzhaf,Mengjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应保护机制，利用特征重要性度量在进化过程中选择性保留已构建的有效特征，防止有价值遗传材料因遗传算子破坏而丢失，在98个回归基准和2个信用分类数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化特征构建在符号回归中表现出潜力，但现有方法缺乏显式机制来保留进化过程中发现的重要构建特征，遗传算子可能破坏有效特征导致有价值遗传材料丢失。

Method: 提出自适应保护机制：利用特征重要性指标对已构建特征进行选择性保护，重要特征获得更强保护，次要特征仍可被修改并可吸收重要特征的构建块。使用多种特征重要性计算方法评估，并在不同基学习器上验证鲁棒性。

Result: 在98个回归基准数据集上，所提机制持续提升解质量优于基线方法；在两个信用分类数据集上的实验表明该方法可有效扩展到符号回归之外的搜索效率改进。

Conclusion: 基于特征重要性度量的自适应保护机制能有效保留进化中的重要构建特征，提升解质量，且在不同基学习器和任务类型上均具有泛化性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Evolutionary feature construction has shown strong promise in symbolic regression by automatically discovering informative transformations of input features that enhance a simple base learner. However, existing approaches often lack explicit mechanisms to preserve important constructed features discovered during evolution, and valuable genetic material can be lost when genetic operators disrupt effective features. This paper introduces an adaptive protection mechanism that leverages feature importance metrics to selectively preserve constructed features during evolution. The mechanism provides stronger protection for more important constructed features while still allowing less important features to be modified and to incorporate useful building blocks from more important features. We evaluate the approach using multiple feature importance calculation methods and demonstrate its robustness across different base learners. Experimental results on 98 regression benchmark datasets show that the proposed mechanism consistently improves solution quality over baseline approaches, and experiments on two credit classification datasets demonstrate that the method also extends effectively to improve search effectiveness beyond symbolic regression.

</details>


### [120] [Training Fair Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14211)
*Patrik Kenfack,Jesse C. Cresswell,Anthony L. Caterini,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Ulrich Aïvodji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairTFM，一种将公平性约束直接整合到表格基础模型（TBM）训练中的方法，通过合成公平性任务和基于梯度反转层的公平性感知架构，使模型能在单次前向传播中实现公平预测。在132个公平性任务上的实验表明，该方法在保持竞争性准确率的同时持续提升了公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFMs）已成为表格预测任务的主流方法，通过上下文学习无需任务特定训练即可预测新数据。尽管TFMs越来越多地应用于高风险的决策场景，但其公平性属性尚未被充分探索。现有公平性技术（1）需要训练数据中敏感属性的访问权限，而这在实际中常受限于；以及（2）与上下文学习范式不兼容。

Method: FairTFM方法包含两个核心组件：（1）基于合成公平性任务的可扩展训练策略，解决训练数据中敏感属性访问受限的问题；（2）利用梯度反转层（Gradient Reversal Layer）实现的公平性感知架构，鼓励模型学习对敏感属性不变的表示。

Result: 在132个公平性任务上进行实验，结果显示公平性指标持续提升，同时保持了具有竞争力的预测准确率。

Conclusion: 通过将公平性约束直接嵌入TFM训练过程，可以在单次前向传播中实现公平预测，验证了在不牺牲准确率的前提下提升模型公平性的可行性。

Abstract: Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have emerged as leading methods for tabular predictive tasks, leveraging in-context learning to predict on new data without task-specific training. Despite the increased use of TFMs in high-stakes decision-making, their fairness properties remain largely unexplored. In this work, we incorporate fairness constraints directly into TFM training, enabling fair predictions in a single forward pass. Our approach addresses two key challenges: limited access to sensitive attributes in training data, and the incompatibility of existing fairness techniques with the in-context learning paradigm. We propose FairTFM, a scalable training strategy based on synthetic fairness tasks and a fairness-aware architecture using a gradient reversal layer, which encourages the model to learn representations invariant to sensitive attributes. Experiments on 132 fairness tasks show consistent improvements in fairness while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [Connected Subspace Clustering: Hardness, a Scalable Heuristic, and an Application to Sea Level Geodesy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14215)
*Johanna Hillebrand,Jan Höckendorff,Jürgen Kusche,Kelin Luo,Heiko Röglin,Melanie Schmidt,Christian Sohler,Bernd Uebbing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连通子空间聚类（Connected Subspace Clustering）问题：给定高维数据点和连通图，将其划分为k个连通簇，最小化各簇到最佳拟合m'维仿射子空间的总平方距离。研究证明该问题即使在m'=0和带孔网格图下也NP-hard且难以近似，随后提出一种高效的Lloyd风格启发式算法（交替子空间拟合与迭代合并强制连通）。在地球物理学的全球海平面时间序列应用中，该方法确保返回恰好k个连通区域，优于无约束方法（可留下多达1,966个不连通碎片），并在160种配置测试中于73.75%的情况下优于四种修复策略中最强的对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的约束优化（如聚类）通常只考虑数据内在的相似性，而忽略了测量位置等物理信息带来的空间连贯性约束。在地球测量学（geodesy）的实际应用中，需要将全球海平面时序观测划分为空间连续的区域以进行主成分分析，但现有方法会产出大量不连通的碎片，缺乏既保证簇内相似、又保证空间物理连续性的高效聚类方法。

Method: 1) 形式化定义Connected Subspace Clustering问题，要求k个连通簇最小化到最佳m'维仿射子空间的总平方距离；2) 证明该问题在m'=0和带孔网格图下NP-hard且不可在Ω(n^{1/2-ε})因子内近似；3) 提出Lloyd风格启发式：交替执行子空间拟合步骤与基于迭代合并的连通性强制步骤（merging-based repair），确保最终输出的k个簇均通过连通图相连。

Result: 在160种全球海平面时序配置的实验中，提出的基于合并的修复策略在73.75%的情况下优于四种策略中的最强对比方法，并在所有测试簇数下持续优于（连通）Ward法等方法。无约束方法会产生高达1,966个不连通碎片且成本更高，而本方法按构造精确返回k个连通区域。识别出的区域与厄尔尼诺-南方涛动（ENSO）和印度洋偶极子（IOD）等气候指数信号一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为带有空间连通性约束的高维子空间聚类提供了理论下界证明与实用启发式求解方案，在保证物理连续性的同时取得优于现有方法的效果。尽管从地球测量学出发，该框架可推广至气候场、遥感、神经影像和传感器网络等其它空间嵌入的多变量时序数据分析场景。

Abstract: Constrained optimization extends classical optimization by integrating side information, making it widely applicable across scientific and engineering domains. Consider a setting where we measure variables at different physical locations. When grouping these measurements, we often want clusters that are both internally similar and physically coherent. Thus, we have a constrained clustering problem where the constraint models coherence. Motivated by an application in geodesy, where contiguous regions of the sea surface must be identified for principal component analysis, we introduce the Connected Subspace Clustering problem: given high-dimensional points and a connectivity graph, partition them into $k$ connected clusters, minimizing their total squared distance to the clusters' best-fit $m'$-dimensional affine subspaces. We prove that, even for $m' = 0$ and a grid graph with holes, the problem is NP-hard to approximate within $Ω(n^{1/2-\varepsilon})$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ is the number of measurements. We then introduce an efficient Lloyd-style heuristic that alternates subspace fitting with an iterative merging procedure to enforce connectivity. Our method returns exactly $k$ connected regions by construction, whereas unconstrained methods leave up to $1{,}966$ disconnected fragments at higher cost. In a study of 160 configurations on global sea level time series, our merging-based repair is the strongest of four strategies in $73.75\%$ of cases, and consistently outperforms competitors such as (connected) Ward's method across all tested cluster counts. The resulting regions isolate signals aligning with climate indices such as the El Nino-Southern Oscillation and Indian Ocean Dipole. Although developed for geodesy, the approach applies to other spatially embedded multivariate time series, such as climate fields, remote sensing, neuroimaging, and sensor networks.

</details>


### [122] [AutoSchema: Live Schema Grounding for Agentic Text-to-Sparql over Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14228)
*Yiming Zhang,Koji Tsuda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSchema，一个无需训练的实时模式接地（live schema grounding）框架，使语言模型代理直接从当前SPARQL端点获取模式证据来查询生命科学知识图谱，无需预建策展的元数据文件，在多个生物医学KGQA任务上优于TogoMCP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学知识图谱通过SPARQL提供大量结构化数据，但每个资源使用不同的模式、标识符和链接。TogoMCP通过提供策展的MIE（Metadata Interoperability Exchange）文件帮助语言模型代理查询这些资源，但创建和维护这些文件仍需要语言模型辅助起草、验证和人工审查，成本高昂且难以扩展。

Method: AutoSchema是一个无需训练的通用实时模式接地框架，包含四个核心步骤：检查实时模式（inspecting live schemas）、将问题中的实体名称映射到图标识符（mapping entity names to graph identifiers）、探索关系路径（exploring relation paths）、在迭代查询构建过程中发现资源之间的可能连接（finding possible connections between resources）。

Result: 在Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA、Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA、Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA（BioASQ Task B）以及化学知识图谱迁移（针对未记录文档的RDF图）四个任务上评估。AutoSchema在生物医学KGQA任务上的平均事实准确性优于TogoMCP，在纵向BioASQ评估中表现一致提升，同时减少了迭代预算耗尽情况，核心评估中平均使用更少的工具调用。迁移研究表明实时模式接地可以支持不规则且未见过的图，无需预先创建策展模式文件。

Conclusion: 实时模式接地是一种可行的替代方案，能够避免策展模式文件的维护成本，支持对未见过的知识图谱进行即时查询，为语言模型代理查询异构SPARQL资源提供了一种更灵活、可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Life science knowledge graphs make large collections of structured data available through SPARQL, but each resource uses its own schema, identifiers, and links. TogoMCP helps language model agents query these resources by providing curated Metadata Interoperability Exchange files. Creating and maintaining these files still requires language model assisted drafting, validation, and manual review. We study \emph{live schema grounding}, where an agent obtains the schema evidence needed for a question directly from the current endpoints. We present \textsc{autoschema}, a general framework for live schema grounding that requires no training. It inspects live schemas, maps entity names in a question to graph identifiers, explores relation paths, and finds possible connections between resources during iterative query construction. We use TogoMCP as our main comparison framework. We evaluate \textsc{autoschema} on Resource Focused Biomedical KGQA, Multi Resource Biomedical KGQA, Longitudinal Biomedical Semantic QA over BioASQ Task B, and Chemistry Knowledge Graph Transfer to a previously undocumented RDF graph. \textsc{autoschema} improves mean factoid accuracy over TogoMCP in the biomedical KGQA tasks and gives consistent gains in the longitudinal BioASQ evaluation. It also reduces iteration budget exhaustion and uses fewer tool calls on average in the core evaluation. The transfer study gives preliminary evidence that live schema grounding can support irregular and previously unseen graphs without first creating a curated schema file.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14264)
*Utkarsh Agarwal,Vamshi Bonagiri,Raul Astudillo,Monojit Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOBO-Merge框架，将模型合并参数选择形式化为黑盒多目标优化问题，利用多目标贝叶斯优化在有限评估预算下逼近帕累托前沿。实验表明在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上，相对于随机搜索在12项对比中11项获得更高平均超体积，特别是在TIES、块级和三目标搜索中收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽能以计算高效方式替代微调，但合并参数选择困难：下游评估昂贵、缺乏梯度信息、源能力间存在冲突。现有方法难以在有限预算下高效选择合并参数。

Method: 将合并参数选择建模为黑盒多目标优化(MOO)问题，引入MOBO-Merge框架，采用多目标贝叶斯优化(MOBO)来逼近帕累托前沿。该框架与合并算子无关，可适配Linear、SLERP、TIES及块级合并算子。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Llama-3.1-8B上，分别于双模型(指令-数学)和三模型(指令-数学-代码)设置下测试。MOBO-Merge在12项对比中11项获得比随机搜索更高的平均超体积；一维线性插值增益较小，而TIES、块级及三目标搜索增益显著。无单一合并算子普遍最优，TIES在四组族-设置组合中三项领先，Block-Linear 4x在Llama三模型合并中最强。

Conclusion: 多目标贝叶斯优化作为表达性合并参数化的搜索层具有实用价值；合并算子的选择需结合具体模型族和任务组合进行，不存在普遍最优的单一合并算子。

Abstract: Model merging combines trained models directly in weight space, offering a compute-efficient alternative to additional fine-tuning. Selecting merge parameters is nevertheless difficult because downstream evaluations are expensive, gradients are unavailable, and source capabilities can conflict. We formulate merge-parameter selection as a black-box multi-objective optimization problem and introduce MOBO-Merge, a merge-operator agnostic framework that uses multi-objective Bayesian optimization to approximate the Pareto front under a limited evaluation budget. We evaluate Qwen3-4B and Llama-3.1-8B in two-model instruction-math and three-model instruction-math-code settings using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and block-wise merge operators. On held-out benchmark partitions, MOBO-Merge obtains higher mean hypervolume than random search in 11 of 12 reported comparisons. The gain is small for one-dimensional Linear interpolation but substantially larger for several TIES, block-wise, and three-objective searches. No merge operator is uniformly best: TIES leads in three of four family-setting combinations, whereas Block-Linear 4x is strongest for the Llama three-model merge. These results show that multi-objective Bayesian optimization is valuable as a search layer for expressive merge parameterizations.

</details>


### [124] [Convex losses and their applications to SVM, SVR, and Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14288)
*Filippo Portera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多个用于二分类任务的新型凸损失函数，适用于SVM和神经网络。由于对偶SVM模型的局限性，作者在原始SVM形式和神经网络中应用这些损失，并采用粒子群优化算法求解原始SVM问题。实验表明这些损失是标准损失的推广，但嵌套交叉验证结果显示泛化性能与传统损失无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过在损失函数中引入模式（pattern）相关性信息来提升SVM和神经网络的泛化性能，以克服传统标准损失函数的局限性。

Method: 提出新的凸损失函数族，证明其为标准损失的推广；在原始SVM形式中使用粒子群优化（PSO）算法求解；在神经网络中直接应用；采用嵌套交叉验证（Nested Cross-Validation）评估每个损失函数的性能，并在多个小型数据集上进行实验。

Result: 提出的损失函数确实是标准损失的推广，但在所测试的小型数据集上，使用新损失与不使用新损失的泛化指标相同，未观察到显著的泛化性能提升。

Conclusion: 这是一项初步研究，表明在损失函数中利用模式相关性在理论上可能增强某些数据集的泛化性能，但实验证据尚未支持这一假设，泛化表现与传统损失持平。

Abstract: We propose multiple new convex losses for SVM and Neural Networks, applied to binary classification tasks. While there are practical limitations in exploiting them with the dual SVM models, we are able to use them with SVM primal formulation and Neural Networks. In detail, the primal SVM problem with the modified losses has been solved with the Particle Swarm Optimization algorithm. We prove that the proposed losses are a generalization of the standard loss, and we experiment them with several small data-sets. This preliminary study shows that using pattern correlations
  inside the loss function could in theory enhance the generalization performances on some data-sets. To evaluate the performance of each loss, we adopt a Nested Cross-Validation procedure. Results show that generalization measures are the same with or without the new losses.

</details>


### [125] [Detecting Contaminated Code-Generation Prompt Batches via Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14303)
*Francesco Quinzan,Noor Munir,Yishun Lu,Stephen Roberts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CodeSIFT 是一种与威胁模型无关的检测方法，利用影响函数识别导致异常代码生成行为的恶意提示批次，在三个开源代码LLM上达到最高0.98的AUROC，无需预先了解攻击类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型用于代码生成时容易受到诱导产生不安全代码的提示攻击。现有防御方法依赖于预定义威胁模型或已知漏洞模式，对新型攻击效果有限，需要一种不依赖具体攻击类别先验知识的通用检测方法。

Method: CodeSIFT 通过影响函数（influence functions）度量生成代码在参数空间中的影响，并利用统计检验判断候选提示集是否偏离良性参考分布，从而识别诱导异常行为的恶意提示批次，而非检测特定漏洞类型。

Result: 在三个3B到7B参数的开源代码LLM上，CodeSIFT达到最高0.98的AUROC（中等至高注入率），保持良好校准的假阳性率，并显著优于静态分析基线。

Conclusion: 基于影响函数的检测方法是不需要先验知识即可识别恶意代码生成提示的有前景方向，为代码LLM安全防护提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet they remain vulnerable to prompts that elicit insecure implementations. Existing defenses typically rely on predefined threat models or known vulnerability patterns, limiting their effectiveness against novel attacks. We propose CodeSIFT, a threat-model-agnostic detection method that leverages influence functions to identify batches of prompts that induce anomalous model behavior. Rather than detecting specific vulnerabilities, CodeSIFT measures the parameter-space influence of generated code and uses a statistical test to determine whether a candidate prompt set deviates from a benign reference distribution. To evaluate our approach, we introduce two benchmark datasets covering a variety of vulnerabilities. We evaluate CodeSIFT on three open-weight code LLMs ranging from 3B to 7B parameters, achieving AUROC scores of up to 0.98 at moderate-to-high injection rates, while maintaining well-calibrated false positive rates and substantially outperforming static analysis baselines. These results suggest that influence-function-based detection is a promising direction for identifying malicious code-generation prompts without requiring prior knowledge of the underlying attack class.

</details>


### [126] [Quantum Multi-Armed Bandits and Linear Bandits: Lower Bounds and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14319)
*Maoli Liu,Zhuohua Li,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为量子多臂老虎机(QMAB)和量子线性老虎机(QLB)建立了首个极小极大遗憾下界：QMAB为Ω(K log(T/K))，有限动作集QLB为Ω(d log(T/d))，解决了Wan等人[2023]提出的"是否存在与T无关的遗憾"问题。同时，作者提出了基于设计的消除算法，将有限动作集QLB的维度依赖从d²改进到d（匹配下界至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作给出了QMAB的O(K log T)遗憾和d维QLB的O(d² polylog T)遗憾，但未解决两个关键问题：K log T的规模是否不可避免？d²的依赖能否改进？这需要下界证明来回答。

Method: 核心证明方法包括：(1) 通过多项式方法和三角函数的多项式Remez型不等式，建立高置信度的单臂量子测试下界；(2) 通过bandit-to-testing归约将单臂下界提升为QMAB下界；(3) 通过线性嵌入获得有限动作集QLB下界。算法方面，设计基于消除的算法，将低偏差低方差量子均值估计器与小支撑G最优设计相结合，通过匹配设计权重的查询分配实现。

Result: 证明了QMAB的Ω(K log(T/K))下界和有限动作集QLB的Ω(d log(T/d))下界。算法方面，当动作集大小为poly(d)时，消除算法遗憾为O(d)，优于先前的d²，匹配下界至多对数因子。设计基于消除的方法可将维度依赖从d²降至d^{3/2}，低方差估计器进一步去除√d因子。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了量子多臂老虎机和有限动作集量子线性老虎机的遗憾复杂度，证明了下界与上界（至多对多数因子）匹配，解决了一个根本性开放问题。

Abstract: We study quantum multi-armed bandits (QMAB) and quantum linear bandits (QLB) in the model of Wan et al. [2023], where the learner queries each arm or action through a quantum reward oracle or its inverse. Prior work gives algorithms over horizon $T$ with regret $O(K\log T)$ for QMAB with $K$ arms and $O(d^2\operatorname{polylog} T)$ for $d$-dimensional QLB. This leaves open whether the $K\log T$ scale is unavoidable and whether the $d^2$ dependence can be improved. We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [127] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的多元轨迹预测方法，通过状态转移表和乘积核检索实现与57M参数Transformer相当的精度，且无需GPU和可学习参数，在数据稀缺场景下表现显著优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的轨迹预测模型（如Transformer）依赖大量训练数据和GPU资源，在数据稀缺的新地理区域难以部署。作者希望提出一种无需训练的替代方案，在保持精度的同时大幅降低资源需求。

Method: 构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表，使用乘积核（基于空间邻近性、方位、速度和时态上下文）进行邻居检索。提供两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成覆盖不同合理路线的轨迹）和波束搜索（寻找最高似然路径）。

Result: 在丹麦海事AIS基准（TrAISformer）上，全数据条件下达到与Transformer相当的精度；在数据稀缺场景下显著优于Transformer，在仅10%训练数据时仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能急剧恶化。

Conclusion: 该方法可在无需GPU训练的情况下以数量级更少的历史数据部署到新地理区域，为轨迹预测提供了一种轻量级、数据高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [128] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地分析了业务流程中延迟检测这一预测性流程监控（PPM）任务的固有难度。通过14个事件日志，发现剩余时间分布通常强烈右偏，现有模型能很好地捕获分布众数但对高延迟案例表现不佳。进一步发现预测不确定性随延迟幅度增加（异方差性），延迟案例的困难根源可能并非类别不平衡，而是更高的不确定性。利用这一相关性可以显著提升延迟案例的识别效果，为PPM研究指明不确定性感知建模方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程中延迟案例的早期检测对组织至关重要，但现有PPM研究多关注通过复杂深度学习架构提升剩余时间预测的总体准确性，对延迟检测本身的固有难度缺乏系统性理解。由于以往评估主要依赖聚合指标，无法揭示模型在目标分布上的表现差异，尤其是对具有较大延迟、操作上最关键的案例，模型表现如何鲜有研究。

Method: 在14个事件日志上对剩余时间预测进行系统性实证分析：首先研究剩余时间的分布特征（偏度、延迟案例比例）；其次评估现有模型在不同延迟程度案例上的预测表现；然后分析预测不确定性与延迟幅度之间的关系（异方差性检验）；最后尝试多种缓解类别不平衡的方法，并探索利用不确定性-延迟相关性来改善延迟案例的识别。

Result: 研究发现：剩余时间分布通常强烈右偏，仅小部分案例呈现较大延迟；现有模型能较好捕获分布众数但在高延迟案例上表现差；预测不确定性随延迟幅度显著增加（存在明显异方差性）；缓解不平衡的方法只能带来有限收益，表明核心问题可能不是不平衡而是高延迟案例固有的更高不确定性；利用不确定性-延迟相关性可以显著改善延迟案例的识别。

Conclusion: 延迟检测困难的根本原因在于高延迟案例伴随的高不确定性，而非简单的类别不平衡。这一发现将不确定性感知建模确定为未来PPM研究的重要方向，为改进延迟案例识别提供了新的理论依据和方法路径。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [129] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出imposter，一种基于物理一致性的判别式自监督预训练任务，通过用其他实体的真实特征替换当前实体子集特征并要求编码器识别交换特征，迫使模型学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。在ERA5-Land再分析数据上使用21个环境变量评估，并在气候分类、碳通量估计和径流预测等七个下游任务上验证，是首个在共享架构和预训练预算下对土地表面建模SSL目标进行系统比较的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据中的实体特征通常受物理定律共同约束，但现有的自监督学习目标大多忽略这种物理一致性。传统的SSL方法仅关注单个特征的内在结构或跨样本的关系，未能利用物理世界赋予特征之间的强耦合关系，因此需要一种能显式引导模型学习跨特征物理依赖的自监督目标。

Method: 提出imposter判别式预训练任务：(1) 从另一实体的真实观测中抽取特征，替换当前实体的子集特征；(2) 由于每个被替换的值在个体上都是真实合理的，模型无法通过简单的单特征统计判断异常，只能通过学习跨特征的物理依赖来识别被替换的特征；(3) 在ERA5-Land数据上使用21个环境变量训练，并在共享架构和预训练预算下与现有SSL目标系统比较。

Result: （1）最有效的预训练任务取决于下游任务家族而非任一单一目标具有全局优势；(2) imposter在与现有SSL目标结合时能提供互补信息；(3) 实验结果支持物理一致性可作为科学基础模型有价值的自监督信号来源。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学数据自监督学习中尚未充分利用的重要信号。imposter通过利用特征间的物理耦合关系为SSL提供了新的方向，且与现有目标互补，为科学基础模型的设计提供了新思路。同时研究也表明，不同下游任务需要不同的预训练策略，不存在放之四海而皆准的单一日标最优解。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [130] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为深度集成的一种经济替代方案，用于在增强数据上训练时实现网络对称性。作者利用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练后期随机轨迹，证明在无限宽度极限下SWA在增强数据上能提供超出SWA本身性能增益的等变性提升，并通过大量实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度集成是利用数据增强将对称性引入神经网络的有效方式，但需要重复训练多次，成本高昂。作者希望找到一种不需要重复训练就能获得类似对称性（等变性）增益的替代方案。

Method: 将SWA作为深度集成的替代方案，并用Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程近似训练末期的随机轨迹，在无限宽度极限下分析SWA在增强数据上训练时的等变性性质。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，SWA在增强数据上提供了超出SWA本身性能提升的等变性增益；大量数值实验（涵盖计算机视觉和图分类，包括离散与连续对称性）验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: SWA是一种无需重复训练即可获得类似深度集成的对称性提升效果的有效方法，其等变性增益在理论上是可保证的并得到了实验验证。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [131] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeaMoE架构，通过将专家按专业领域分组并共享大部分参数，配合两阶段路由策略，显著减少小批量解码场景下MoE模型的专家权重加载开销，实现最高50.9%的加载量减少和2倍峰值加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在实时交互场景（如编码助手、实时音视频交互）中广泛使用，这些场景要求极低响应延迟，因此常采用小批量解码。但小批量解码下MoE推理受限于内存带宽，严重受专家权重加载瓶颈制约。现有解决方案（后训练权重压缩、预训练阶段细粒度专家设计）要么降低模型精度，要么引入额外计算和通信开销，缺乏针对该瓶颈的有效解决方法。

Method: 提出DeaMoE架构，将专家划分为多个"部门"（departments），同一部门的专家来自相同专业领域并共享大部分参数，每个专家仅保留少量私有参数以体现其独特性。同时设计定制化的两阶段路由策略，避免冗余的权重加载。

Result: 与vanilla MoE相比，DeaMoE在LLM解码阶段每步加载权重减少最高50.9%；在A40上对预训练7B模型实现最高1.33倍端到端TPOT加速；在微基准测试中，对DeepSeek-V3在A40和H100上分别实现2.00倍和1.97倍的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE通过参数共享的部门化专家设计加两阶段路由策略，有效缓解了小批量解码下MoE模型的内存带宽瓶颈，在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅提升解码效率，为MoE在延迟敏感场景的部署提供了新的架构方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [132] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CytoBERT，一个用于流式细胞术数据的开源基础模型。该模型通过自监督预训练在包含15个人类数据集、超过5000万个细胞的语料库上学习跨标记物关系，并通过标记物标准化处理异构面板，实现了跨异质数据集的转移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞术数据在不同实验方案和标记物选择下高度异质且缺乏标准化，使得传统机器学习方法难以跨研究应用和迁移。尽管基础模型在其他领域取得了进展，但在细胞术领域相关方法仍十分匮乏。

Method: CytoBERT采用BERT架构，通过标记物标准化对15个人类数据集（含异构标记物面板、超5000万细胞）进行大规模语料构建，以自监督方式进行预训练，学习细胞内部可迁移的标记物间关系；随后在下游样本级分类任务上进行微调。

Result: 样本级分类的微调实验表明，跨异质细胞术数据集的转移学习是可行的，CytoBERT提供了可扩展、可泛化的细胞术分析基础。模型为公开可用、开源、开放权重。

Conclusion: CytoBERT验证了基础模型框架在单细胞流式细胞术数据上的适用性，为处理可变标记物面板和跨数据集迁移提供了可复现的解决方案，可作为大规模、通用细胞术分析的起点。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [133] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个悖论：Power Sampling 虽能使概率质量更集中于正确轨迹，却会损害其下游推理性能（自一致性场景下准确率最高下降18.5个百分点）。作者将其归因于剂量失配（固定指数导致分布变化幅度不均衡）与覆盖失配（全局锐化使概率集中于少数主导路径，尽管pass@k高但仍丢失了推理所需的广泛路径支持）。据此提出一种变形控制且保持支持集的Power目标来校准锐化并限制中等概率路径的抑制，在同预算的加权自一致性下逆转了损失并超越标准多采样推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling作为一种无验证器的推理增强方法，理论上应能通过锐化生成轨迹分布来提升推理质量，同时可作为多种下游采样方法的通用前端。但作者发现了令人惊讶的悖论：尽管Power Sampling将更多概率质量推向正确轨迹，却反而损害了它本应增强的下游推理性能。理解并解决这一悖论是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者首先通过实验识别出两个失配根源：（1）剂量失配——固定指数在不同问题上引发差异巨大的分布变化程度；（2）覆盖失配——全局锐化将质量集中于少数主导路径，即使pass@k保持高值，也丢失了下游聚合/搜索/选择所需的广泛推理路径支持。基于此诊断，作者提出用受变形控制的、保持支持集的Power目标替代均匀轨迹指数化，实现跨问题的锐化校准，同时抑制对中等概率路径的过度压制。

Result: 在同预算的加权自一致性（weighted self-consistency）应用中，修复后的采样器能够逆转全局Power采样造成的性能损失，并在多种推理基准上超越标准多采样推理方法。原始Power Sampler在自一致性下导致最高18.5个百分点的准确率下降。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的悖论源于未对分布变形量进行控制且未保持推理路径的覆盖广度。通过变形控制和支持保持的锐化目标，可以在保留Power锐化优势的同时避免其对下游推理的负面影响，使Power Sampling真正成为通用的推理增强前端。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过连续松弛替代离散的神经元级和激活级决策，衍生出三种可扩展的NAS方法（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA），在MLP、CNN、RNN和Transformer等多种架构上验证了其在识别紧凑且高性能架构方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS天然被表述为双层优化问题，但由于离散的架构决策、指数级增长的搜索空间以及候选架构训练的高成本，NAS在计算上非常昂贵。现有方法难以在可扩展性与搜索质量之间取得平衡，亟需一种通用的、可微分的优化框架来高效地进行架构搜索。

Method: 将NAS形式化为双层优化问题，上层优化架构（基于验证性能），下层训练网络参数（基于训练损失）。提出三种可扩展的公式化方法，用连续松弛替代离散的神经元级（neuron gating）和激活级（mixed activation）决策，使原本组合式的架构空间可进行可微优化，从而衍生出NAS-NG、NAS-MA和NAS-NGMA三种方法。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的MLP和CNN实验中，所提方法一致地识别出紧凑且预测性能具有竞争力或更优的架构。MNIST上NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%测试精度，NAS-NG以仅0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%精度；CIFAR-10上一致优于vanilla DARTS。此外，NAS-NG还能优化过参数化和文献最优架构，在减少参数的同时提升精度。

Conclusion: 松弛化的双层优化被证实是离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为高效的神经元级和激活级架构优化提供了一个通用框架。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [135] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的可微分神经架构搜索框架。LP-NAS利用验证损失梯度和训练损失Hessian构建线性规划，计算既能提升泛化能力又保持模型参数最优性的架构更新方向，并衍生出S-LP-NAS和R-LP-NAS两种高效变体。在DARTS搜索空间上实现S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS，相比标准DARTS收敛更快、早期搜索验证性能更高，在CIFAR-10/100及ImageNet上均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分NAS将架构搜索空间松弛为连续域，从而可以应用连续优化原理，但现有方法未能充分利用数学规划工具来同时保证泛化性能和参数最优性，需要更高效的架构优化方法。

Method: LP-NAS构建以验证损失梯度与训练损失Hessian为输入的线性规划问题，求解出既能改善泛化又不破坏模型参数最优性的架构更新方向。提出两个计算高效变体S-LP-NAS和R-LP-NAS，并将它们适配到DARTS搜索空间得到S-LP-DARTS和R-LP-DARTS。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上，LP-DARTS在架构搜索和评估阶段均优于标准DARTS；与P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS等变体对比中展现了有效性；在ImageNet上验证了发现架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 基于线性规划的NAS框架能够更高效地导航架构搜索空间，实现更快的收敛和更高的验证性能，为可微分NAS提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [136] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出sMuon（small Muon），一种将Muon优化器适配到低秩参数高效微调（PEFT）场景的方法。由于LoRA的低秩参数化无法直接正交化权重更新，作者通过线性化和最小二乘方法求解松弛的Muon目标，仅使用矩阵乘法实现高效计算。实验表明sMuon在SFT和ReLoRA预训练中表现良好，带来中等的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在神经网络预训练中相比其他优化器有明显优势，但在参数高效微调（PEFT）中应用较少。主要原因是主流的PEFT方法LoRA的低秩参数化在数学上无法对权重更新进行正交化，导致Muon无法自然与LoRA结合。本文旨在解决这一适配难题，使Muon的优势能扩展到低秩微调场景。

Method: 作者通过线性化将低秩设定下的松弛Muon目标近似求解，并利用最小二乘方法进行优化。实现上仅使用矩阵乘法（matmul）操作，避免使用更复杂的线性代数分解例程，从而保证计算效率。该方法被命名为sMuon（small Muon）。

Result: sMuon在监督微调（SFT）和ReLoRA预训练实验中均表现良好。虽然具体效果取决于模型和评测基准，但总体而言，使用Muon进行低秩微调能带来中等程度的性能提升。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Muon优化器适配到低秩PEFT场景中，提出的sMuon方法兼具数学可行性与计算效率，为低秩微调提供了新的优化选择，并验证了Muon在PEFT领域的适用性。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [137] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段跨表格数据生成（CTDG）框架，用于从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型并生成新的合成异构表格。第一阶段将异构原始表转换为同构的统计表，第二阶段用扩散Transformer模型学习并生成统计表，再通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建原始表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据生成方法主要局限于单输入表格场景，无法有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格。因此需要一种能够从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段将每个异构原始表转换为标准化的统计表，所有表共享相同的列，捕获原始列的边际分布及两两相关性；第二阶段使用扩散Transformer（Diffusion Transformer）模型在同构统计表上学习结构模式并生成合成统计表，然后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在学习统计表示上具有较高的保真度，在生成合成数据上取得了良好的保真度-多样性权衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架成功解决了多异构表格的联合生成问题，支持从多个异构表格中学习统一生成模型，并能生成无限数量的逼真合成异构表格。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [138] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex 是一个针对视觉语言模型（VLM）强化学习（RL）后训练阶段优化的运行时系统。它将参考评分和训练阶段中的 prefix（视频/提示前缀）计算重叠到 rollout 解码窗口内，并通过阶段感知的内存管理和并行度感知的权重共享，在 32 张 H800 GPU 上实现 1.23x–1.30x（对比串行同置）和 1.57x–2.24x（对比解耦）的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy RL 运行时将 rollout、reference scoring 和 actor training 以严格串行阶段执行。对于 VLM，处理密集视频输入和 prompt 前缀占据大量计算时间，且前缀计算与生成的响应无关，可以在 rollout 解码窗口内并行执行，避免 GPU 算力闲置。当前串行方案浪费了这些可重叠的计算能力。

Method: Rollplex 提出两个核心机制：(1) 阶段感知的内存管理（phase-aware memory management）：根据 producer-consumer 生命周期控制 HBM 驻留，使参考和训练阶段的 prefix 计算可与 rollout 解码同时进行；(2) 并行度感知的权重共享（parallelism-aware weight sharing）：在不同 TP 度之间复用兼容张量的同一物理存储，仅重建不兼容的张量，避免完整的第二份 actor 副本，从而在有限的 HBM（如 Qwen2.5-VL-32B 需约 165 GiB/GPU）约束下实现并发调度。

Result: 在 32 张 H800 GPU 上，Rollplex 相较于串行同置方案获得 1.23x–1.30x 加速，相较于相同 GPU 预算下的解耦（disaggregation）方案获得 1.57x–2.24x 加速，同时保持同步 RL 更新的语义。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 的 prefix 计算从 reference 和 training 阶段中解耦并重叠到 rollout 解码窗口，能够在不破坏同步 on-policy RL 语义的前提下显著提升 GPU 利用率；阶段感知内存管理和并行度感知权重共享是使该重叠调度在实际 VLM 内存约束下可行的关键技术。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [139] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RecipeNet是一种层次化Transformer架构，用于配方数据（如材料合成、药物配方、工业制造中的有序步骤序列）的表示学习。它通过堆叠的Transformer编码器分别捕获步骤内的字段级交互和步骤间的序列依赖，在多个配方数据集和任务上优于现有表格学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据涉及多个领域（材料合成、药物配方、工业制造），其过程表示为包含异构结构化字段的有序步骤序列。现有表格学习方法通常将这些结构扁平化为固定模式表示，无法有效捕获层次化字段交互和过程依赖关系。

Method: RecipeNet采用层次化Transformer架构：底层Transformer编码器建模每个步骤内的字段级交互，上层Transformer编码器建模步骤间的顺序依赖，通过堆叠实现从步骤内到步骤间的多层次表示学习。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet一致性地优于现有表格模型，验证了层次化和序列化建模对配方表示学习的价值。

Conclusion: RecipeNet证明了针对配方数据定制层次化序列建模架构的有效性，为处理异质结构化过程数据提供了比通用表格学习更强的方法。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Refined outer-approximation algorithms for monotonic optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13694)
*Ahmed Rashwan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对单调优化问题中的多面体外逼近算法（POA）提出三项算法改进：广义锚点选择（实现最优平衡单调性割）、松弛最优性条件（无需连续性假设即可保证有限终止）、以及向量化变体（并发处理多个节点）。同时开发了基于树的高效实现来加速核心子程序并紧凑存储外逼近。实验表明这些改进相比标准POA实现数量级加速，并能解决现有变体无法处理的问题。作者还发布了开源Python包polyblocks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调优化是一类广泛的非凸问题，常用POA算法求解，但POA扩展性差：描述逼近所需顶点数可能指数增长，导致内存需求大且子程序代价高昂。

Method: 提出三项算法改进：(1) 广义锚点选择，产生最优平衡单调性割；(2) 松弛最优性条件，无需连续性假设即可保证有限终止；(3) 向量化变体，并发处理多个节点。并设计基于树的高效实现，加速核心子程序并紧凑存储外逼近。

Result: 数值实验表明改进后的算法相比标准POA实现数量级加速，并能解决现有变体失败的问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法改进和高效实现显著提升了POA的可扩展性与实用性，并已通过开源Python包polyblocks向社区推广。

Abstract: Monotonic optimisation is a broad class of non-convex problems formulated in terms of monotone functions. Such problems are commonly solved via the polyblock outer-approximation algorithm (POA), a branch-and-bound method that iteratively refines a rectangular outer-approximation of the feasible set. POA scales poorly, however: the number of vertices needed to describe the approximation can grow exponentially, leading to large memory requirements and increasingly expensive subroutines. To address these limitations, we propose three algorithmic improvements: a generalised anchor selection that yields an optimal balanced monotonicity cut, a relaxed optimality condition that guarantees finite termination without continuity assumptions, and a vectorised variant that processes multiple nodes concurrently. We further develop an efficient tree-based implementation, which accelerates POA's core subroutines while storing the outer-approximation compactly. Numerical experiments show that these improvements yield order-of-magnitude speed-ups over standard POA and solve problems on which existing variants fail. Finally, we introduce polyblocks, an open-source Python package implementing the proposed algorithms alongside a framework for developing new POA variants, available at https://github.com/RashwanA/polyblocks.

</details>


### [141] [Multi-period Value-at-Risk Constrained Portfolio Optimization via DC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13748)
*Thi Thu Van Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带有限场景VaR约束、交易成本和分散化正则化的多期投资组合优化问题。利用有限场景VaR-CVaR恒等式，将该问题转化为凸投资组合集上的惩罚DC（差凸）规划问题。提出投影惯性Boosted DC函数算法（iBDCA），结合惯性外推、目标保护和加速线搜索，证明了算法的良定义性、目标序列单调下降性以及收敛到惩罚DC问题临界点的性质。在局部无平局条件下，建立整序列收敛及局部R-线性收敛速率。数值实验与DCA和标准BDCA对比，并通过样本外回测与等权、买入持有基准比较，展示收益、风险、交易成本与VaR控制之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多期投资组合优化中同时考虑VaR约束、交易成本和分散化正则化会导致非光滑、非凸的优化问题，现有算法在收敛性和求解效率上存在不足，需要设计兼具理论保障和实际有效性的求解算法。

Method: 利用有限场景VaR-CVaR恒等式将原问题转化为惩罚DC规划形式；提出投影惯性Boosted DC函数算法（iBDCA），融合惯性外推、目标保护机制和boosted线搜索三类加速技术；从理论上分析算法的收敛性质，并通过数值实验和样本外回测验证算法性能。

Result: 证明了iBDCA算法良定义、目标函数值单调递减、迭代序列的聚点均为惩罚DC问题的临界点；在局部无平局条件下序列整体收敛且具有局部R-线性收敛速度。数值实验表明iBDCA优于DCA和标准BDCA；样本外回测显示了不同基准下收益、风险、交易成本与VaR控制的权衡关系。

Conclusion: iBDCA为带VaR约束的多期组合优化提供了一种同时具备全局收敛保证和快速实际收敛性能的高效求解方法，能够有效平衡收益、风险与交易成本等多目标需求。

Abstract: We study a multi-period portfolio optimization problem with finite-scenario Value-at-Risk (VaR) constraints, transaction costs, and diversification regularization. Using a finite-scenario VaR--CVaR identity, we derive a penalized difference-of-convex (DC) formulation over the underlying convex portfolio set. To solve the resulting nonsmooth and nonconvex problem, we propose a projected inertial Boosted Difference-of-Convex Functions Algorithm (iBDCA) that combines inertial extrapolation, an objective safeguard, and a boosted line search. We prove that the method is well defined, generates a monotonically decreasing objective sequence, and has only critical accumulation points for the penalized DC problem. Under a local no-ties condition at an accumulation point, we further establish whole sequence convergence with a local \(R\)-linear rate. Numerical experiments compare iBDCA with DCA and standard BDCA on matched problem instances. Out-of-sample backtests additionally include equal-weight and buy-and-hold benchmarks to illustrate the trade-offs among realized return, risk, transaction costs, and empirical VaR control.

</details>


### [142] [Best Response Dynamics for Zero-Sum Dynamic Games with Partial-Asymmetric Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13755)
*Yuxiang Guan,Iman Shames,Tyler H. Summers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究部分非对称信息下的零和随机线性二次动态博弈（LQDGs），提出基于最佳响应动力学的求解方法，推导了纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略下每方玩家的最佳响应闭式解，并通过数值实验发现高阶信念状态对博弈值的影响迅速衰减，仅需少量迭代即可收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分非对称信息给零和随机线性二次动态博弈带来了根本性挑战，包括信念表示（belief representation）和心智理论（theory of mind）等难题。现有工作表明，传统动态规划式的分解方法难以直接应用于此类问题，因此需要探索替代性求解范式。

Method: 提出基于最佳响应动力学（best response dynamics）的替代方法。在纯线性动态输出反馈控制策略类中，推导了每个玩家最佳响应的显式表达式，其中每个控制器的内部状态维度是系统状态维度的整数倍。玩家通过迭代更新最佳响应，逐步形成更高阶的信念状态，导致内部控制状态在理论上趋于无限维。

Result: 数值实验表明，博弈值在仅经过少量迭代后即收敛，说明高阶信念状态带来的收益可忽略不计。此外，通过数值实验系统分析了非对称信念、信念阶数、相对可控性与可观测性以及直接馈通（direct feed-through）对线性二次追逃博弈值的影响。

Conclusion: 最佳响应动力学为部分非对称信息下的LQDG提供了一种可行的求解框架，且实践中有限阶信念即足以逼近博弈值，高阶信念的边际收益趋于零，这为实际应用中降低计算复杂度提供了理论依据。

Abstract: This work studies a class of zero-sum stochastic linear quadratic dynamic games (LQDGs) under partial and asymmetric information. Information asymmetry introduces fundamental challenges related to \textit{belief representation} and \textit{theory of mind}, where players must impute belief states and estimates of other players to inform their strategies. Existing work highlights the difficulty of applying dynamic programming-like decomposition approach to these problems. An alternative approach based on \textit{best response dynamics} is proposed, which provides insights into belief representation and theory of mind challenges. Explicit expressions for each player's best response within the class of pure linear dynamic output feedback control strategies are derived, where the internal state dimension of each control is an integer multiple of the system state dimension. As players iteratively update their best responses, they form increasingly higher-order belief states, leading to infinite-dimensional internal states. However, numerical results reveal that the game's value converges after only a few iterations, suggesting that higher-order belief states provide vanishing benefit. This work further conducts numerical experiments to analyze the impact of asymmetric beliefs, belief orders, relative controllability and observability, and direct feed-through on a linear quadratic pursuit-evasion game's value.

</details>


### [143] [Recursive Filtering and Stochastic Control under Finite Partition-Based Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13825)
*Saul Díaz-Infante Velasco,Yofre H. Garcia,Jesús Adolfo Minjárez-Sosa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于部分可观测随机系统的滤波与最优控制框架，其中每个观测对应隐藏状态空间有限可测划分中的一个类别，覆盖了阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等机制。在测度层面构造类别受限的未归一化条件测度，通过归一化得到后验分布，并据此引入由观测类别及其上条件测度构成的信息状态，将原问题转化为完全可观测的马尔可夫决策过程，推导了Bellman方程并给出平稳最优策略存在性条件，同时提出类别相关的有限维近似方案及滤波误差与值函数误差的抽象界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测随机系统的控制问题中，区域化信息机制（如阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测）广泛存在且具有实际意义，但缺乏统一的数学框架来进行滤波与最优控制。现有方法通常针对特定观测结构分别处理，难以建立统一的证明和近似方法，因此需要一个能够统一刻画这类"类别观测"结构并支持理论分析与计算逼近的通用框架。

Method: 在测度层面对每个可观测类别定义类别受限的未归一化条件测度，通过其预测概率归一化获得后验分布。引入由观测类别和该类别上的条件测度共同构成的信息状态，将部分可观测问题转化为完全可观测的MDP。推导折扣成本准则下的Bellman方程，建立平稳最优策略存在条件；针对无穷维信息空间提出类别相关的有限维近似（保留连续分量与原子质量），并推导近似滤波器误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界。

Result: 建立了统一的滤波与最优控制框架，覆盖多种区域化观测机制；证明了平稳最优策略的存在性条件；提出了类别相关的有限维近似方案，可同时保留连续分量和原子质量；导出了近似滤波误差与值函数误差之间的抽象界；通过直方图近似的参考模型验证了该构造的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有区域化（类别化）观测机制的部分可观测随机系统提供了一个统一的测度级框架，既有理论完备性（最优策略存在性、Bellman方程），又有计算可行性（有限维近似及误差界），为阈值、量化、删失、事件触发和间歇观测等实际场景的最优控制提供了通用方法论基础。

Abstract: We develop a filtering and optimal-control framework for partially observable stochastic systems in which each observation identifies a class of a finite measurable partition of the hidden state space. This structure covers regional-information mechanisms associated with threshold, quantized, censored, event-triggered, and intermittent observations, and allows observable classes with atomic, continuous, or mixed components. The formulation is constructed first at the level of measures: for each observable class, we define a class-restricted unnormalized conditional measure, and the posterior distribution is obtained by normalizing with its predictive probability. Based on this recursion, we introduce an information state consisting of the observed class and the conditional measure supported on it, thereby transforming the original problem into a fully observable Markov decision process. We formulate the discounted-cost criterion, derive the Bellman equation, and establish conditions for the existence of stationary optimal policies. To address the infinite-dimensional nature of the information space, we propose class-dependent finite-dimensional approximations capable of preserving both continuous components and atomic masses. We also derive an abstract bound linking the error of the approximate filter to the error of the value function. A reference model illustrates the construction through histogram-based approximations

</details>


### [144] [Nondegeneracy Conditions for Control Problems with Nonregular Mixed Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13834)
*Jorge Becerril,Karla Cortez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带单个非正则混合不等式约束的最优控制问题，建立了非退化性和正态性条件。作者首先证明在单个时刻非正则的模型问题中，退化是内在且不可避免的——每个容许过程都通过纯电荷以退化方式满足必要条件，且自然正则逼近的归一化乘子在L∞对偶空间中无弱*极限。基于此分析，提出四个可验证的条件，逐步保证非退化乘子、纯电荷消失、正态性及电荷质量的显式界。此外，在非正则终端时刻，一个平衡恒等式将电荷质量与成本乘子联系起来，决定完全异常与正态之间的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非正则混合不等式约束（如路径约束在单个时刻退化为等式）导致最优性必要条件中出现纯有限可加集函数（电荷）作为乘子，这使得经典的正态性和非退化性分析失效。作者旨在理解这种退化结构的本质，并提供可验证的条件来恢复非退化性和正态性保证。

Method: 首先构建一个在单时刻非正则的模型问题，证明退化是内在的——所有容许过程均以纯电荷退化满足必要条件。进而分析了自然正则逼近的乘子序列在L∞弱*拓扑下的极限行为，证明仅有Banach极限意义下纯电荷出现。基于该分析，提出四个递进的可验证条件（条件C1-C4），分别保证非退化乘子的存在、纯电荷消逝、正态性以及电荷质量的显式上界。对非正则终端时刻，建立了平衡恒等式，连接电荷质量与成本乘子。

Result: 证明了单点非正则问题中退化性的不可避免性，即不存在任何容许过程能以非退化方式满足经典必要条件；正则逼近的归一化乘子在紧化意义下仅产生纯电荷。提出的四个条件逐步确保非退化性、纯电荷消除、正态性和电荷质量界。终端时刻的平衡恒等式揭示了电荷质量与成本乘子之间的定量关系。

Conclusion: 非正则混合约束所导致的电荷乘子并非病态伪象，而是该问题的固有结构特征。通过四个可验证条件可以系统性地恢复经典最优性理论的非退化性和正态性保证，终端时刻的平衡恒等式为判断完全异常提供了精确判据。

Abstract: We establish nondegeneracy and normality conditions for optimal control problems with a single nonregular mixed inequality constraint, where purely finitely additive set functions (charges) appear as multipliers. We first prove for a model problem nonregular at a single instant that degeneration is intrinsic and unavoidable: every admissible process satisfies the necessary conditions degenerately via a pure charge, and normalized multipliers of natural regular approximations admit no weak$^*$ limit in $L^\infty([0,1];\mathbb{R})^*$, yielding only pure charges under every generalized limit, in the sense of Banach limits. Motivated by this analysis, we propose four verifiable conditions that progressively guarantee nondegenerate multipliers, the vanishing of the pure charge, normality, and explicit bounds on charge mass. Furthermore, we show that at a nonregular terminal instant, a balance identity ties the charge mass to the cost multiplier, deciding between complete abnormality and normality. We provide examples confirming the realizability and discussing the limits of each condition.

</details>


### [145] [First-Order Optimization as Minimum-Time Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13915)
*Liraz Mudrik,Isaac Kaminer,Pramod P. Khargonekar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文将一阶优化重新表述为一个最小时间控制问题：迭代点为状态，由已观测梯度组成的更新为控制，梯度范数满足容差的点构成目标集。对固定目标函数和初始点，达到目标所需的最少查询次数构成一个值函数，衡量实例本身的复杂度而非最坏情况复杂度。在强凸二次函数上，共轭梯度迭代从离散Pontryagin条件中自然涌现，值函数成为可控性指标；超越二次情形，Hessian生成的可达张成空间替代可控性矩阵，可行性成为可达性问题。曲率被视为一种资源，可验证条件能证明实例所需步数少于其二次模型的可控性指标，且差距可随维度无界增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化复杂度分析关注最坏情况下的收敛率（如经典Nesterov界），但这种方法无法刻画单个具体实例的内在难度。作者希望建立一种从控制理论视角出发的实例级复杂度度量，使每个优化问题都能被精确地评估其"固有难度"，从而为任意一阶方法提供基准，而不是笼统的类别级最坏情况保证。

Method: 将一阶优化建模为最小时间最优控制问题：状态为迭代点，控制为基于历史梯度组合的更新，目标集为梯度范数不超过容差的点集。通过离散Pontryagin极大值原理推导最优控制条件，在强凸二次情形下自然得到共轭梯度算法。利用可控性理论中的可控性指标（controllability index）量化复杂度；在非二次情形，引入Hessian生成的可达张成空间（Hessian-generated reachable span）取代可控性矩阵，将可行性转化为可达性问题，并以可验证条件判断步数是否可少于二次模型的可控性指标。

Result: 在强凸二次函数上，从离散Pontryagin条件涌现出共轭梯度迭代，值函数即为可控性指标。在一般（非二次）情形，求解可行性归约为判断临界点是否在Hessian生成的可达张成空间内可达到，以及需要多少步。作者给出可验证条件，证明某些实例所需步数可少于其二次模型在极小值点处的可控性指标，且该差距可随维度无界增长，表明曲率信息确实能被算法利用为一种资源。

Conclusion: 该框架首次将实例级复杂度（而非最坏情形复杂度）通过控制论的Pontryagin条件和可控性理论进行刻画，建立了优化算法设计与最优控制之间的深层联系。值函数可作为评估任意一阶方法在具体实例上表现的基准，为实例自适应算法设计和复杂度下界分析提供新的理论工具。

Abstract: We formulate first-order optimization as a minimum-time control problem. The iterate is the state, the update, a combination of the gradients observed so far, is the control, and the points with gradient norm at most a prescribed tolerance form the target set. For a fixed objective and start, the minimum number of oracle queries needed to reach the target is a value function: it measures the complexity of the instance, not the worst case over a class. On a strongly convex quadratic, the conjugate gradient iterates emerge from the discrete Pontryagin conditions, and the value is a controllability index. Beyond the quadratic, a Hessian-generated reachable span replaces the controllability matrix, and feasibility becomes a reachability question: whether a critical point can be reached at all, and in how many steps, is read from the span. Curvature is a resource: a checkable condition certifies that an instance needs fewer steps than the controllability index of its quadratic model at the minimizer, and the gap can grow without bound with the dimension. The value is thus a benchmark for the intrinsic difficulty of each instance, against which any first-order method can be measured.

</details>


### [146] [Benders Decomposition with Partial Non-Anticipativity Relaxation for Multi-Stage Stochastic Clean Energy Transition Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14105)
*Ahmet Emir Şener,Burak Kocuk,Tuğçe Yüksel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了校园级电-热综合能源系统在清洁能源转型规划中的问题，提出了一个多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，结合鲁棒优化处理短期运行不确定性，并开发了带部分非预期性松弛的Benders分解算法高效求解，案例验证表明该方法在合理计算预算内可求解高时间分辨率的实际问题，且鲁棒性提升能显著改善运行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校园级电-热综合能源系统的清洁能源转型规划同时面临战略层面（投资决策）和运行层面（需求与可再生能源波动）的双重不确定性，现有方法要么无法处理大规模高时间分辨率问题，要么缺乏对运行不确定性的稳健考虑，因此需要一种能够同时考虑多阶段投资决策与短期运行不确定性的高效求解方法。

Method: 本文提出一个多阶段随机混合整数规划模型，联合优化可再生能源发电、储能和热传输技术的投资与运行决策。为处理短期运行不确定性，基于盒式不确定集推导鲁棒重构；求解方面开发了Benders分解算法，采用部分非预期性松弛——投资变量及其非预期性约束保留在主问题中，运行变量分配至情景子问题并松弛其非预期性约束，终止时通过一个较小的线性规划恢复非预期性，并证明该修正步骤只会使目标函数值以有限界增加。此外引入有效不等式和两阶段割添加策略加速收敛。

Result: 以中东技术大学校园为案例，所提方法能在合理计算预算内以高时间分辨率求解，而传统广泛形式或经典Benders分解方法无法实现。通过滚动时域蒙特卡洛模拟和样本外分析评估投资方案，结果表明加入鲁棒性可以显著提高转型方案的运行可靠性，同时总成本仅有适度增加。

Conclusion: 所提出的部分非预期性松弛Benders分解算法能够有效解决多阶段随机混合整数规划在校园能源转型问题中的大规模计算瓶颈，且鲁棒优化框架在提升运行可靠性与控制成本之间取得了良好平衡，为校园级综合能源系统规划提供了实用的决策支持工具。

Abstract: We study clean energy transition planning for campus-scale integrated electricity-heat systems under both strategic level and operational level uncertainties. We formulate a multi-stage stochastic mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes investment and operational decisions for renewable generation, storage, and heat-transfer technologies whose costs and efficiencies evolve stochastically across stages. To account for short-term operational uncertainty, we further derive a robust reformulation based on box uncertainty sets for demand, renewable generation, and heat-transfer performance. To solve the resulting large-scale model, we develop a Benders decomposition algorithm with partial non-anticipativity relaxation. Investment variables and their non-anticipativity constraints are retained in the master problem while operational variables are assigned to scenario-wise subproblems where their non-anticipativity constraints are relaxed. Non-anticipativity of operational variables is restored only at termination through a smaller linear program. We prove that this correction step can only increase the objective function value by a finite bound. We improve the computational performance of the algorithm developed with valid inequalities and a two-phase cut-addition strategy. Using the proposed approach, we solve the Middle East Technical University campus case study at high temporal resolution within a reasonable computational budget, which is not otherwise possible with the extensive form or the classical Benders decomposition. We evaluate the resulting investment plans through rolling-horizon Monte Carlo simulations with an out-of-sample analysis and demonstrate that the added robustness can significantly improve the operational reliability of the transition plans with a moderate increase in total cost.

</details>


### [147] [Joint Inventory Placement, Assortment Personalization, and Order Fulfillment for Substitutable Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14275)
*Mikhail Fadin,Omar El Housni,Huseyin Topaloglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在线零售商在分布式仓储网络中联合优化库存分配、个性化选品和订单履约的问题，提出了具有理论保证的近似算法。在一般概率选择模型下，算法在仓库容量和产品供给规模扩展时具有渐近最优性；在MNL选择模型下，获得了常数因子近似算法，近似因子从最一般设定下的0.080到平稳且供应无限设定下的0.199。这是首个针对该联合问题提供可证明高效算法的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线零售商依赖分布式仓储网络快速履行客户订单，但库存分配、个性化选品和订单履约这三个决策在现实中高度耦合，此前缺乏统一的优化框架。现有研究通常孤立处理各子问题，未解决三者联合优化所带来的复杂性与理论保障难题。

Method: 将问题建模为一个多阶段决策问题：销售期开始时先决定各仓库的库存分配，随后在有限时间内按已知分布到达不同类型的顾客，顾客根据离散选择模型在给定选品集合中选择产品，最后企业从可行仓库中选择一个来履行订单，同时消耗库存并获得与产品、顾客类型和仓库选择相关的利润。针对一般概率选择模型和MNL模型，分别设计了具有渐近最优性和常数因子近似保证的近似算法与策略。

Result: 在一般概率选择模型下，算法在仓库容量和产品供给规模增长时达到渐近最优；在MNL模型下，获得常数因子近似算法，近似因子为0.080（最一般设定）至0.199（平稳且产品供应无限、仅存在仓库容量约束的设定）。数值实验表明算法具有强经验性能。

Conclusion: 该研究是首个为库存分配、选品个性化和订单履约的联合决策问题提供可证明高效算法的成果，填补了该领域理论分析的空白，为在线零售商的运营决策提供了兼具理论保障与实践有效性的求解方法。

Abstract: Modern online retailers leverage networks of distributed warehouses to rapidly fulfill customer orders. We study a problem of jointly deciding (i) how to allocate inventories across warehouses in the network subject to warehouse capacity and product supply constraints, (ii) how to dynamically select personalized product assortments based on customer preferences and location, as well as real-time stock levels, and (iii) which warehouse to use to fulfill the product chosen by the customer to maximize expected profit. In our model, the firm chooses an inventory placement across warehouses at the start of the selling horizon. Customers of different types then arrive at discrete time periods over a finite time horizon according to a known distribution. When offered an assortment, each customer type chooses at most one product according to a discrete choice model. The firm then chooses a feasible warehouse from which to fulfill the chosen product, depleting its stock and earning a profit that depends on the product, customer type, and warehouse choice. We develop approximation algorithms and policies with provable theoretical guarantees and strong empirical performance. Under general probabilistic choice models, we establish asymptotically optimal approximation algorithms as warehouse capacities and product supplies scale. Under the multinomial logit (MNL) choice model, we obtain constant-factor approximation algorithms, with approximation factors ranging from 0.080 in the most general setting to 0.199 under stationarity and unlimited product supplies, which means that only warehouse capacity constraints are present. To the best of our knowledge, these results provide the first provably efficient algorithms for the joint inventory placement, assortment personalization, and order fulfillment problem.

</details>


### [148] [Optimizing bounds for energy-constrained optimal cooling problems in two dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14334)
*Pedro Blöss Braga,Giovanni Fantuzzi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究二维不可压缩流体在具有冷边界和内热源/热汇条件下的最优冷却控制问题。通过拉格朗日对偶将冷却效率上界问题转化为良态对偶问题，并用半定规划层次进行数值逼近。作者证明了任意域和热分布下效率界为Pe²量级，且在圆盘和环带中具有正方位角平均热分布时界可改进为Pe²/ln²Pe。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二维不可压缩流体的最优冷却问题，目的是在给定动能预算（以无量纲Péclet数衡量）下最大化冷却效率。需要建立严格的数学框架来求得上界，并推广和改进已有的圆盘内能量约束冷却流的效率界。

Method: 利用拉格朗日对偶性构造良态的对偶问题，通过离散化获得收敛的半定规划层次来数值逼近对偶问题；利用问题对称性降低计算复杂度；通过构造对偶问题的可行点获得解析上界。

Result: 在任意域和热分布下证明ε(Pe) ≲ Pe²；在圆盘和环带中，当热源/热汇分布具有正方位角平均时，证明ε(Pe) ≲ Pe²/ln²Pe。这些结果推广并改进了已知的圆盘冷却流效率界。同时提供了正方形和环带域上的数值计算结果。

Conclusion: 通过拉格朗日对偶和半定规划相结合的方法，成功建立了二维冷却流最优效率的通用上界框架，给出了新的解析界并推广到更一般的域和热分布情形，改进并推广了已有结果。

Abstract: We study optimal control problems for incompressible fluids in two-dimensional domains with a cold boundary and internal heat sources and sinks. Given a kinetic energy budget, measured by the square of a nondimensional Péclet number $\mathrm{Pe}$, the goal is to maximize a cooling efficiency inversely proportional to the mean square gradient of the fluid's temperature. Using Lagrange duality, we formulate a well-posed dual problem whose solution yields an upper bound on the maximum cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$. We then numerically approximate the dual problem using a convergent hierarchy of semidefinite programs obtained via discretization. We illustrate this computational approach on optimal cooling problems in a square and in an annulus, explaining also how problem symmetries can be exploited to reduce computational complexity. Finally, we construct admissible points for the dual problem to obtain new analytical upper bounds on the optimal cooling efficiency $\mathcal{E}(\mathrm{Pe})$.
